        self._position_history: list[int] = []

        self.reset()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Zobrist
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _init_zobrist(self) -> None:
        rng = random.Random(123)
        self._zobrist_table = [
            [[rng.getrandbits(64) for _ in range(self._num_piece_types)] for _ in range(self.size)]
            for _ in range(self.size)
        ]

    def _update_zobrist(self, row: int, col: int, old_val: int, new_val: int) -> None:
        if old_val != self.empty_value and old_val < self._num_piece_types:
            self._zobrist_hash ^= self._zobrist_table[row][col][old_val]
        if new_val != self.empty_value and new_val < self._num_piece_types:
            self._zobrist_hash ^= self._zobrist_table[row][col][new_val]

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Lifecycle
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def reset(self) -> None:
        self._board = [[self.empty_value for _ in range(self.size)] for _ in range(self.size)]
        self._move_stack.clear()
        self._redo_stack.clear()
        self._zobrist_hash = 0
        self._position_history.clear()

    def copy(self) -> IntersectionBoard:
        new_board = IntersectionBoard(
            self.size,
            self.empty_value,
            num_piece_types=self._num_piece_types,
        )
        new_board._board = [row[:] for row in self._board]
        new_board._zobrist_hash = self._zobrist_hash
        new_board._zobrist_table = self._zobrist_table
        new_board._position_history = list(self._position_history)
        return new_board

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Cell Access
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def get_cell(self, row: int, col: int) -> int:
        return self._board[row][col]

    def set_cell(self, row: int, col: int, value: int) -> bool:
        if not self.is_valid_position(row, col):
            return False
        old = self._board[row][col]
        self._board[row][col] = value
        self._update_zobrist(row, col, old, value)
        return True

    def get_intersection(self, row: int, col: int) -> int:
        """Alias for ``get_cell`` in intersection terminology."""
        return self.get_cell(row, col)

    def set_intersection(self, row: int, col: int, value: int) -> bool:
        """Alias for ``set_cell`` in intersection terminology."""
        return self.set_cell(row, col, value)

    def is_valid_position(self, row: int, col: int) -> bool:
        return 0 <= row < self.size and 0 <= col < self.size

    def is_cell_empty(self, row: int, col: int) -> bool:
        if not self.is_valid_position(row, col):
            return False
        return self._board[row][col] == self.empty_value

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Stone-specific methods
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def place_stone(self, row: int, col: int, player: int) -> bool:
        """Place a stone at ``(row, col)`` if empty.

        Args:
            row: Row index.
            col: Column index.
            player: Player id (stone colour).

        Returns:
            ``True`` if placed.
        """
        if not self.is_cell_empty(row, col):
            return False
        return self.set_cell(row, col, player)

    def remove_stone(self, row: int, col: int) -> bool:
        """Remove a stone from ``(row, col)``.

        Args:
            row: Row index.
            col: Column index.

        Returns:
            ``True`` if a stone was removed.
        """
        if self.is_cell_empty(row, col):
            return False
        return self.set_cell(row, col, self.empty_value)

    def count_stones(self, player: int) -> int:
        """Count stones belonging to *player*."""
        count = 0
        for row in self._board:
            for cell in row:
                if cell == player:
                    count += 1
        return count

    def get_player_positions(self, player: int) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:
        """Return all positions occupied by *player*."""
        positions: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
        for r in range(self.size):
            for c in range(self.size):
                if self._board[r][c] == player:
                    positions.append((r, c))
        return positions

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Superko Detection
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def push_position(self) -> None:
        """Save the current Zobrist hash to the position history."""
        self._position_history.append(self._zobrist_hash)

    def is_superko_violation(self) -> bool:
        """Check if the current position has occurred before.

        Returns:
            ``True`` if this exact board state already exists in history.
        """
        return self._zobrist_hash in self._position_history

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Board Queries
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def get_board_state(self) -> list[list[int]]:
        return [row[:] for row in self._board]

    def get_board_hash(self) -> int:
        return self._zobrist_hash

    def is_board_full(self) -> bool:
        for row in self._board:
            for cell in row:
                if cell == self.empty_value:
                    return False
        return True

    def get_dimensions(self) -> tuple[int, int]:
        return (self.size, self.size)

    def get_available_moves(self) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:
        moves: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
        for r in range(self.size):
            for c in range(self.size):
                if self._board[r][c] == self.empty_value:
                    moves.append((r, c))
        return moves

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Make / Undo
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def make_move(
        self,
        row: int,
        col: int,
        value: int,
        *,
        captured: list[tuple[int, int, int]] | None = None,
        extra: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> bool:
        """Place a stone and record the move for undo.

        Args:
            row: Row index.
            col: Column index.
            value: Player id.
            captured: List of ``(r, c, old_value)`` for captured stones.
            extra: Any additional undo data.

        Returns:
            ``True`` if placed.
        """
        if not self.is_valid_position(row, col):
            return False

        record: dict[str, Any] = {
            "row": row,
            "col": col,
            "old_value": self._board[row][col],
            "new_value": value,
            "captured": captured or [],
        }
        if extra:
            record["extra"] = extra

        self.set_cell(row, col, value)
        self._move_stack.append(record)
        self._redo_stack.clear()
        return True

    def undo_move(self) -> bool:
        if not self._move_stack:
            return False
        record = self._move_stack.pop()

        # Restore the placed stone
        self.set_cell(record["row"], record["col"], record["old_value"])

        # Restore captured stones
        for r, c, old_val in record.get("captured", []):
            self.set_cell(r, c, old_val)

        # Pop position history
        if self._position_history:
            self._position_history.pop()

        self._redo_stack.append(record)
        return True

    def redo_move(self) -> bool:
        if not self._redo_stack:
            return False
        record = self._redo_stack.pop()

        # Re-remove captured stones
        for r, c, _ in record.get("captured", []):
            self.set_cell(r, c, self.empty_value)

        self.set_cell(record["row"], record["col"], record["new_value"])
        self.push_position()
        self._move_stack.append(record)
        return True

    def can_undo(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self._move_stack)

    def can_redo(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self._redo_stack)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Dunder
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        symbols = {self.empty_value: ".", 1: "●", 2: "○"}
        lines: list[str] = []
        for row in self._board:
            lines.append(" ".join(symbols.get(c, str(c)) for c in row))
        return "\n".join(lines)

    def __eq__(self, other: object) -> bool:
        if not isinstance(other, IntersectionBoard):
            return NotImplemented
        return self._board == other._board

    def __hash__(self) -> int:
        return self._zobrist_hash

#engine/ai/evaluation.py
# engine/ai/evaluation.py
"""Shared evaluation framework for board game heuristics.

Provides a base ``Evaluator`` class and common evaluation building
blocks (material counting, mobility, centre control) that game-specific
evaluators compose into a full position evaluation.

Usage::

    class ChessEvaluator(Evaluator):
        def evaluate(self, state):
            score  = self.material_balance(state)
            score += self.piece_square_tables(state)
            score += self.mobility_score(state)
            return score
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from typing import Any


class Evaluator(ABC):
    """Abstract base for game-specific position evaluators.

    Each game module subclasses this and implements ``evaluate()``.
    The shared helpers below cover the most common heuristic patterns.
    """

    @abstractmethod
    def evaluate(self, state: Any) -> float:
        """Return a numeric evaluation of the position.

        Positive values favour the maximising player;
        negative values favour the minimiser.

        Args:
            state: Game state (board + metadata).

        Returns:
            Evaluation score as a float.
        """

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Common Heuristic Helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @staticmethod
    def material_count(
        board: Any,
        piece_values: dict[int, float],
        player: int,
        opponent: int,
    ) -> float:
        """Sum the material value of pieces on the board.

        Args:
            board: Board object with ``get_cell(r, c)`` and
                ``get_dimensions()``.
            piece_values: Mapping from piece id to numeric value.
            player: The maximising player id.
            opponent: The minimising player id.

        Returns:
            ``player_score - opponent_score``
        """
        dims = board.get_dimensions()
        rows, cols = dims[0], dims[1] if len(dims) > 1 else dims[0]

        player_score = 0.0
        opponent_score = 0.0

        for r in range(rows):
            for c in range(cols):
                piece = board.get_cell(r, c)
                value = piece_values.get(piece, 0.0)
                if value > 0:
                    # Determine ownership by checking piece ranges
                    # This is a generic helper — subclass should override
                    # for accuracy
                    player_score += value

        return player_score - opponent_score

    @staticmethod
    def mobility_score(
        legal_moves_player: int,
        legal_moves_opponent: int,
        weight: float = 0.1,
    ) -> float:
        """Evaluate based on the number of legal moves available.

        More moves = more options = better position.

        Args:
            legal_moves_player: Count of legal moves for the current player.
            legal_moves_opponent: Count for the opponent.
            weight: Multiplier for the mobility difference.

        Returns:
            Weighted mobility difference.
        """
        return weight * (legal_moves_player - legal_moves_opponent)

    @staticmethod
    def centre_control_score(
        board: Any,
        player: int,
        rows: int,
        cols: int,
        weight: float = 0.3,
    ) -> float:
        """Reward pieces closer to the centre of the board.

        Args:
            board: Board object.
            player: Player id.
            rows: Board rows.
            cols: Board columns.
            weight: Multiplier.

        Returns:
            Centre control bonus.
        """
        centre_r = rows / 2.0
        centre_c = cols / 2.0
        max_dist = max(centre_r, centre_c)
        if max_dist == 0:
            return 0.0

        score = 0.0
        for r in range(rows):
            for c in range(cols):
                if board.get_cell(r, c) == player:
                    dist = abs(r - centre_r) + abs(c - centre_c)
                    score += weight * (1.0 - dist / (2.0 * max_dist))
        return score

    @staticmethod
    def piece_square_value(
        board: Any,
        tables: dict[int, list[list[float]]],
        rows: int,
        cols: int,
    ) -> float:
        """Apply piece-square tables for positional evaluation.

        Args:
            board: Board object.
            tables: Mapping from piece id to a 2-D table of bonus values.
            rows: Board rows.
            cols: Board columns.

        Returns:
            Total positional bonus.
        """
        score = 0.0
        for r in range(rows):
            for c in range(cols):
                piece = board.get_cell(r, c)
                table = tables.get(piece)
                if table is not None and r < len(table) and c < len(table[r]):
                    score += table[r][c]
        return score

    @staticmethod
    def connectivity_score(
        positions: list[tuple[int, int]],
        weight: float = 0.05,
    ) -> float:
        """Reward clusters of connected pieces (adjacency bonus).

        Args:
            positions: List of ``(row, col)`` positions for one player.
            weight: Per-adjacency bonus.

        Returns:
            Total connectivity score.
        """
        pos_set = set(positions)
        score = 0.0
        for r, c in positions:
            for dr, dc in [(-1, 0), (1, 0), (0, -1), (0, 1)]:
                if (r + dr, c + dc) in pos_set:
                    score += weight
        return score / 2.0  # Each pair counted twice

#engine/ai/heuristics.py
# engine/ai/heuristics.py
"""Common heuristic functions shared across game AI modules.

These are stateless helper functions that game-specific evaluators
call from their ``evaluate()`` method.  They cover patterns that
appear in multiple games (line threats, capture threats, territory).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Line / Sequence Heuristics  (TTT, Connect4, Gomoku, Pente)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def count_lines_of_length(
    board: Any,
    player: int,
    length: int,
    rows: int,
    cols: int,
    directions: list[tuple[int, int]],
    empty_value: int = 0,
) -> int:
    """Count how many open lines of *length* the player has.

    An "open line" is *length* consecutive player pieces that can
    still be extended (at least one open end).

    Args:
        board: Board object with ``get_cell(r, c)``.
        player: Player id to count for.
        length: Required consecutive count.
        rows: Board rows.
        cols: Board columns.
        directions: List of ``(dr, dc)`` direction vectors to scan.
        empty_value: Value for empty cells.

    Returns:
        Number of qualifying lines found.
    """
    count = 0
    visited: set = set()

    for r in range(rows):
        for c in range(cols):
            if board.get_cell(r, c) != player:
                continue
            for dr, dc in directions:
                key = (r, c, dr, dc)
                if key in visited:
                    continue

                # Count consecutive pieces in this direction
                consecutive = 0
                cr, cc = r, c
                while 0 <= cr < rows and 0 <= cc < cols and board.get_cell(cr, cc) == player:
                    consecutive += 1
                    cr += dr
                    cc += dc

                if consecutive != length:
                    continue

                # Check for open ends
                # Before
                br, bc = r - dr, c - dc
                open_before = (
                    0 <= br < rows and 0 <= bc < cols and board.get_cell(br, bc) == empty_value
                )
                # After
                open_after = (
                    0 <= cr < rows and 0 <= cc < cols and board.get_cell(cr, cc) == empty_value
                )

                if open_before or open_after:
                    count += 1
                    # Mark visited to avoid double counting
                    for i in range(consecutive):
                        visited.add((r + i * dr, c + i * dc, dr, dc))

    return count


def line_threat_score(
    board: Any,
    player: int,
    win_length: int,
    rows: int,
    cols: int,
    directions: list[tuple[int, int]],
    empty_value: int = 0,
) -> float:
    """Evaluate based on line threats (near-wins).

    Assigns exponentially increasing scores for longer lines.

    Args:
        board: Board object.
        player: Player id.
        win_length: Length needed to win.
        rows: Board rows.
        cols: Board columns.
        directions: Direction vectors.
        empty_value: Empty cell value.

    Returns:
        Threat score (higher = stronger position).
    """
    score = 0.0
    for length in range(2, win_length + 1):
        count = count_lines_of_length(board, player, length, rows, cols, directions, empty_value)
        # Exponential weighting: 3-in-a-row is worth much more than 2-in-a-row
        weight = 10.0 ** (length - 1)
        score += count * weight
    return score


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Capture Heuristics  (Checkers, Chess, Pente)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def capture_advantage(
    player_pieces: int,
    opponent_pieces: int,
    weight: float = 1.0,
) -> float:
    """Score based on piece count advantage.

    Args:
        player_pieces: Number of pieces the player has.
        opponent_pieces: Number the opponent has.
        weight: Multiplier.

    Returns:
        Weighted difference.
    """
    return weight * (player_pieces - opponent_pieces)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Territory Heuristics  (Go, Nine Men's Morris)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def territory_score(
    player_territory: int,
    opponent_territory: int,
    weight: float = 1.0,
) -> float:
    """Score based on territory control.

    Args:
        player_territory: Player's controlled territory.
        opponent_territory: Opponent's controlled territory.
        weight: Multiplier.

    Returns:
        Weighted territory difference.
    """
    return weight * (player_territory - opponent_territory)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Safety / King Safety (Chess)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def king_safety_penalty(
    king_exposure: int,
    weight: float = -0.5,
) -> float:
    """Penalise exposed kings.

    Args:
        king_exposure: Number of attacking lines towards the king.
        weight: Negative multiplier (penalty).

    Returns:
        Penalty score.
    """
    return weight * king_exposure


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Pawn Structure (Chess)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def pawn_structure_score(
    doubled_pawns: int,
    isolated_pawns: int,
    passed_pawns: int,
) -> float:
    """Evaluate pawn structure quality.

    Args:
        doubled_pawns: Count of doubled pawns (bad).
        isolated_pawns: Count of isolated pawns (bad).
        passed_pawns: Count of passed pawns (good).

    Returns:
        Structure score.
    """
    return -0.5 * doubled_pawns - 0.5 * isolated_pawns + 1.0 * passed_pawns


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Mill Heuristics (Nine Men's Morris)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def mill_count_score(
    player_mills: int,
    opponent_mills: int,
    weight: float = 5.0,
) -> float:
    """Score based on number of mills formed.

    Args:
        player_mills: Player's mill count.
        opponent_mills: Opponent's mill count.
        weight: Multiplier.

    Returns:
        Weighted mill advantage.
    """
    return weight * (player_mills - opponent_mills)


def potential_mill_score(
    player_potential: int,
    opponent_potential: int,
    weight: float = 2.0,
) -> float:
    """Score for "almost mills" (2-of-3 with empty third).

    Args:
        player_potential: Player's potential mills.
        opponent_potential: Opponent's potential mills.
        weight: Multiplier.

    Returns:
        Weighted advantage.
    """
    return weight * (player_potential - opponent_potential)

#engine/ai/mcts.py
# engine/ai/mcts.py
"""Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) with UCT selection.

Used by the IMPOSSIBLE difficulty tier for Go, and optionally for
other games where deep Minimax is too slow (large branching factor).

Usage::

    mcts = MCTSEngine(
        get_moves=go_get_moves,
        apply_move=go_apply,
        is_terminal=go_is_terminal,
        get_result=go_get_result,   # +1 win, -1 loss, 0 draw
    )
    best_move = mcts.search(state, iterations=2000)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import random
import time
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

from engine.utils.constants import AI_MCTS_EXPLORATION_CONSTANT

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Type aliases
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

GetMovesFunc = Callable[[Any], list[Any]]
ApplyMoveFunc = Callable[[Any, Any], Any]
IsTerminalFunc = Callable[[Any], bool]
GetResultFunc = Callable[[Any, int], float]  # (state, player) → reward


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# MCTS Node
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class MCTSNode:
    """A node in the MCTS game tree.

    Attributes:
        state: The game state at this node.
        parent: Parent node (``None`` for root).
        move: The move that led to this node.
        player: The player who made that move.
        children: List of child nodes.
        untried_moves: Moves not yet expanded.
        visits: Number of times this node was visited.
        wins: Accumulated reward from this node's perspective.
    """

    __slots__ = (
        "state",
        "parent",
        "move",
        "player",
        "children",
        "untried_moves",
        "visits",
        "wins",
    )

    def __init__(
        self,
        state: Any,
        parent: MCTSNode | None = None,
        move: Any = None,
        player: int = 0,
        untried_moves: list[Any] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.state = state
        self.parent = parent
        self.move = move
        self.player = player
        self.children: list[MCTSNode] = []
        self.untried_moves: list[Any] = untried_moves or []
        self.visits: int = 0
        self.wins: float = 0.0

    def uct_value(self, exploration: float = AI_MCTS_EXPLORATION_CONSTANT) -> float:
        """Upper Confidence bound for Trees (UCT).

        Args:
            exploration: Exploration constant (default √2).

        Returns:
            UCT score.
        """
        if self.visits == 0:
            return float("inf")
        parent_visits = self.parent.visits if self.parent else 1
        exploitation = self.wins / self.visits
        exploration_term = exploration * math.sqrt(math.log(parent_visits) / self.visits)
        return exploitation + exploration_term

    def is_fully_expanded(self) -> bool:
        """Return ``True`` if all moves have been tried."""
        return len(self.untried_moves) == 0

    def best_child(self, exploration: float = AI_MCTS_EXPLORATION_CONSTANT) -> MCTSNode:
        """Return the child with the highest UCT value.

        Args:
            exploration: UCT exploration constant.

        Returns:
            Best child node.
        """
        return max(self.children, key=lambda c: c.uct_value(exploration))

    def most_visited_child(self) -> MCTSNode | None:
        """Return the child with the most visits (for final move selection).

        Returns:
            Most-visited child, or ``None``.
        """
        if not self.children:
            return None
        return max(self.children, key=lambda c: c.visits)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# MCTS Engine
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


@dataclass
class MCTSStats:
    """Statistics from a MCTS search."""

    iterations: int = 0
    time_elapsed: float = 0.0
    max_depth: int = 0


class MCTSEngine:
    """Game-agnostic Monte Carlo Tree Search engine.

    Attributes:
        get_moves: Returns legal moves for a state.
        apply_move: Applies a move and returns a new state.
        is_terminal: Returns ``True`` if the game is over.
        get_result: Returns reward (+1 win, -1 loss, 0 draw) for a
            player given a terminal state.
        get_current_player: Returns the player to move in a state.
        exploration: UCT exploration constant.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        get_moves: GetMovesFunc,
        apply_move: ApplyMoveFunc,
        is_terminal: IsTerminalFunc,
        get_result: GetResultFunc,
        get_current_player: Callable[[Any], int] | None = None,
        exploration: float = AI_MCTS_EXPLORATION_CONSTANT,
        copy_state: Callable[[Any], Any] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        from copy import deepcopy

        self.get_moves = get_moves
        self.apply_move = apply_move
        self.is_terminal = is_terminal
        self.get_result = get_result
        self.get_current_player = get_current_player
        self.exploration = exploration
        self.copy_state = copy_state or (lambda s: s.copy() if hasattr(s, "copy") else deepcopy(s))
        self._stats = MCTSStats()

    def search(
        self,
        state: Any,
        player: int,
        iterations: int = 1000,
        time_limit: float = 0.0,
    ) -> Any | None:
        """Run MCTS and return the best move.

        Args:
            state: Current game state.
            player: The player who is about to move.
            iterations: Maximum number of simulations.
            time_limit: Time limit in seconds (0 = unlimited).

        Returns:
            Best move found, or ``None``.
        """
        start_time = time.perf_counter()
        self._stats = MCTSStats()

        moves = self.get_moves(state)
        if not moves:
            return None

        root = MCTSNode(
            state=state,
            player=player,
            untried_moves=list(moves),
        )

        for i in range(iterations):
            if time_limit > 0:
                if time.perf_counter() - start_time >= time_limit:
                    break

            # 1. Selection
            node = self._select(root)

            # 2. Expansion
            if not self.is_terminal(node.state) and node.untried_moves:
                node = self._expand(node)

            # 3. Simulation (rollout)
            result = self._simulate(node.state, player)

            # 4. Backpropagation
            self._backpropagate(node, result, player)

            self._stats.iterations = i + 1

        self._stats.time_elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time

        # Return the move of the most-visited child
        best = root.most_visited_child()
        return best.move if best else None

    @property
    def stats(self) -> MCTSStats:
        """Return stats from the most recent search."""
        return self._stats

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # MCTS Phases
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _select(self, node: MCTSNode) -> MCTSNode:
        """Descend the tree using UCT until a non-fully-expanded
        or terminal node is reached."""
        while node.is_fully_expanded() and node.children:
            if self.is_terminal(node.state):
                break
            node = node.best_child(self.exploration)
        return node

    def _expand(self, node: MCTSNode) -> MCTSNode:
        """Expand one untried move and return the new child node."""
        move = node.untried_moves.pop(random.randrange(len(node.untried_moves)))
        child_state = self.apply_move(self.copy_state(node.state), move)
        child_moves = self.get_moves(child_state)

        # Determine child player
        child_player = node.player
        if self.get_current_player is not None:
            child_player = self.get_current_player(child_state)

        child = MCTSNode(
            state=child_state,
            parent=node,
            move=move,
            player=child_player,
            untried_moves=child_moves,
        )
        node.children.append(child)
        return child

    def _simulate(self, state: Any, root_player: int) -> float:
        """Random playout from *state* until the game ends.

        Args:
            state: Starting state for the rollout.
            root_player: The player from whose perspective we score.

        Returns:
            Reward for the root player.
        """
        current = self.copy_state(state)
        depth = 0
        max_rollout = 500  # Safety limit

        while not self.is_terminal(current) and depth < max_rollout:
            moves = self.get_moves(current)
            if not moves:
                break
            move = random.choice(moves)
            current = self.apply_move(current, move)
            depth += 1

        self._stats.max_depth = max(self._stats.max_depth, depth)
        return self.get_result(current, root_player)

    def _backpropagate(
        self,
        node: MCTSNode,
        result: float,
        root_player: int,
    ) -> None:
        """Propagate the simulation result up the tree."""
        current: MCTSNode | None = node
        while current is not None:
            current.visits += 1
            # From this node's player perspective
            if current.player == root_player:
                current.wins += result
            else:
                current.wins -= result
            current = current.parent

#engine/ai/minimax.py
# engine/ai/minimax.py
"""Minimax search with Alpha-Beta pruning, move ordering, and iterative deepening.

This module provides a generic, game-agnostic Minimax engine.
Game-specific AI subclasses supply:
    - ``get_legal_moves(state)``  → list of moves
    - ``apply_move(state, move)`` → new state (or mutate + return)
    - ``evaluate(state)``         → float score (higher = better for maximiser)
    - ``is_terminal(state)``      → bool

Usage::

    engine = MinimaxEngine(
        get_moves=chess_get_moves,
        apply=chess_apply,
        evaluate=chess_evaluate,
        is_terminal=chess_is_terminal,
    )
    best_move = engine.search(state, depth=5)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import time
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

from engine.ai.transposition_table import TranspositionTable, TTFlag

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Type aliases for the game-specific callbacks
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

GetMovesFunc = Callable[[Any], list[Any]]
ApplyMoveFunc = Callable[[Any, Any], Any]
UndoMoveFunc = Callable[[Any, Any], None]
EvaluateFunc = Callable[[Any], float]
IsTerminalFunc = Callable[[Any], bool]
MoveOrderFunc = Callable[[Any, list[Any]], list[Any]]


@dataclass
class SearchStats:
    """Statistics from a single search invocation.

    Attributes:
        nodes_evaluated: Total leaf nodes evaluated.
        depth_reached: Maximum depth explored.
        time_elapsed: Wall-clock seconds.
        cutoffs: Number of alpha-beta pruning cutoffs.
    """

    nodes_evaluated: int = 0
    depth_reached: int = 0
    time_elapsed: float = 0.0
    cutoffs: int = 0


class MinimaxEngine:
    """Game-agnostic Minimax with Alpha-Beta pruning.

    Supports both copy-based and in-place (make/undo) state management.

    Attributes:
        get_moves: Returns legal moves for a state.
        apply_move: Applies a move and returns the new state.
        undo_move: Undoes a move in-place (optional — if ``None``,
            ``apply_move`` must return an independent copy).
        evaluate: Heuristic evaluation of a state.
        is_terminal: Returns ``True`` if the game is over.
        order_moves: Optional move ordering for better pruning.
        transposition_table: Optional TranspositionTable for memoization.
        get_hash: Optional function computing an integer hash for a state.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        get_moves: GetMovesFunc,
        apply_move: ApplyMoveFunc,
        evaluate: EvaluateFunc,
        is_terminal: IsTerminalFunc,
        undo_move: UndoMoveFunc | None = None,
        order_moves: MoveOrderFunc | None = None,
        transposition_table: TranspositionTable | None = None,
        get_hash: Callable[[Any], int] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.get_moves = get_moves
        self.apply_move = apply_move
        self.undo_move = undo_move
        self.evaluate = evaluate
        self.is_terminal = is_terminal
        self.order_moves = order_moves
        self.transposition_table = transposition_table
        self.get_hash = get_hash

        self._stats = SearchStats()
        self._time_limit: float = 0.0
        self._start_time: float = 0.0
        self._timed_out: bool = False

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Public API
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def search(
        self,
        state: Any,
        depth: int = 4,
        maximise: bool = True,
        time_limit: float = 0.0,
    ) -> Any | None:
        """Find the best move using Alpha-Beta Minimax.

        Args:
            state: Current game state.
            depth: Maximum search depth.
            maximise: ``True`` if the current player is the maximiser.
            time_limit: Wall-clock time limit in seconds (0 = unlimited).

        Returns:
            Best move found, or ``None``.
        """
        self._stats = SearchStats()
        if time_limit > 0:
            self._time_limit = time_limit
            self._start_time = time.perf_counter()
            self._timed_out = False
        elif self._time_limit <= 0:
            self._time_limit = 0.0
            self._start_time = time.perf_counter()
            self._timed_out = False

        moves = self.get_moves(state)
        if not moves:
            return None

        if self.transposition_table is not None:
            self.transposition_table.generation += 1

        if self.order_moves:
            moves = self.order_moves(state, moves)

        best_move: Any | None = None
        best_score = float("-inf") if maximise else float("inf")

        for move in moves:
            if self._check_timeout():
                break

            child = self._do_move(state, move)
            score = self._alpha_beta(
                child,
                depth - 1,
                float("-inf"),
                float("inf"),
                not maximise,
            )
            self._do_undo(state, move)

            if maximise:
                if score > best_score:
                    best_score = score
                    best_move = move
            else:
                if score < best_score:
                    best_score = score
                    best_move = move

        self._stats.time_elapsed = time.perf_counter() - self._start_time
        self._stats.depth_reached = depth
        return best_move

    def search_iterative(
        self,
        state: Any,
        max_depth: int = 10,
        maximise: bool = True,
        time_limit: float = 5.0,
    ) -> Any | None:
        """Iterative deepening — searches depth 1, 2, …, *max_depth*.

        Returns the best move from the deepest completed search.

        Args:
            state: Current game state.
            max_depth: Maximum depth to reach.
            maximise: ``True`` if current player is the maximiser.
            time_limit: Total time budget in seconds.

        Returns:
            Best move found.
        """
        self._start_time = time.perf_counter()
        self._time_limit = time_limit
        self._timed_out = False

        best_move: Any | None = None

        for depth in range(1, max_depth + 1):
            if self._check_timeout():
                break
            move = self.search(state, depth, maximise, time_limit=0)
            if not self._timed_out and move is not None:
                best_move = move
            if self._timed_out:
                break

        return best_move

    @property
    def stats(self) -> SearchStats:
        """Return statistics from the most recent search."""
        return self._stats

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Core Algorithm
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _alpha_beta(
        self,
        state: Any,
        depth: int,
        alpha: float,
        beta: float,
        maximise: bool,
    ) -> float:
        """Recursive Alpha-Beta search with Transposition Table memoization.

        Args:
            state: Current game state.
            depth: Remaining depth.
            alpha: Lower bound.
            beta: Upper bound.
            maximise: ``True`` for maximising player.

        Returns:
            Evaluation score.
        """
        if self._check_timeout():
            return self.evaluate(state)

        if depth <= 0 or self.is_terminal(state):
            self._stats.nodes_evaluated += 1
            return self.evaluate(state)

        orig_alpha = alpha
        orig_beta = beta

        # Transposition table probe
        hash_key = None
        if self.transposition_table is not None:
            if self.get_hash is not None:
                hash_key = self.get_hash(state)
            elif hasattr(state, "get_hash"):
                hash_key = state.get_hash()
            elif hasattr(state, "get_zobrist_hash"):
                hash_key = state.get_zobrist_hash()
            elif isinstance(state, dict) and "hash" in state:
                hash_key = state["hash"]

            if hash_key is not None:
                cached_score = self.transposition_table.lookup_score(hash_key, depth, alpha, beta)
                if cached_score is not None:
                    self._stats.cutoffs += 1
                    return cached_score

        moves = self.get_moves(state)
        if not moves:
            self._stats.nodes_evaluated += 1
            return self.evaluate(state)

        if self.order_moves:
            moves = self.order_moves(state, moves)
        elif self.transposition_table is not None and hash_key is not None:
            best_tt_move = self.transposition_table.get_best_move(hash_key)
            if best_tt_move is not None and best_tt_move in moves:
                moves = [best_tt_move] + [m for m in moves if m != best_tt_move]

        best_move_local = None

        if maximise:
            value = float("-inf")
            for move in moves:
                child = self._do_move(state, move)
                score = self._alpha_beta(child, depth - 1, alpha, beta, False)
                self._do_undo(state, move)
                if score > value:
                    value = score
                    best_move_local = move
                alpha = max(alpha, value)
                if alpha >= beta:
                    self._stats.cutoffs += 1
                    break
        else:
            value = float("inf")
            for move in moves:
                child = self._do_move(state, move)
                score = self._alpha_beta(child, depth - 1, alpha, beta, True)
                self._do_undo(state, move)
                if score < value:
                    value = score
                    best_move_local = move
                beta = min(beta, value)
                if alpha >= beta:
                    self._stats.cutoffs += 1
                    break

        # Transposition table store
        if self.transposition_table is not None and hash_key is not None:
            if value <= orig_alpha:
                flag = TTFlag.UPPER
            elif value >= orig_beta:
                flag = TTFlag.LOWER
            else:
                flag = TTFlag.EXACT
            self.transposition_table.store(hash_key, depth, value, flag, best_move=best_move_local)

        return value

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _do_move(self, state: Any, move: Any) -> Any:
        """Apply a move — either copy-based or in-place."""
        return self.apply_move(state, move)

    def _do_undo(self, state: Any, move: Any) -> None:
        """Undo a move in-place if ``undo_move`` is provided."""
        if self.undo_move is not None:
            self.undo_move(state, move)

    def _check_timeout(self) -> bool:
        """Return ``True`` if the time limit has been exceeded."""
        if self._time_limit <= 0:
            return False
        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - self._start_time
        if elapsed >= self._time_limit:
            self._timed_out = True
            return True
        return False

#engine/ai/random_ai.py
# engine/ai/random_ai.py
"""Random move AI — the simplest possible player.

Used as the BEGINNER fallback and as a baseline for testing.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import random
from typing import Any


class RandomAI:
    """Select a uniformly random legal move.

    This is a stateless helper — it doesn't implement ``AIInterface``
    directly; instead game-specific ``AIBase`` subclasses call it
    from their ``_select_beginner_move`` hook.

    Usage::

        move = RandomAI.select(legal_moves)
    """

    @staticmethod
    def select(legal_moves: list[Any]) -> Any | None:
        """Pick a random move from the list.

        Args:
            legal_moves: Non-empty list of valid moves.

        Returns:
            A randomly chosen move, or ``None`` if list is empty.
        """
        if not legal_moves:
            return None
        return random.choice(legal_moves)

    @staticmethod
    def select_weighted(
        legal_moves: list[Any],
        weights: list[float],
    ) -> Any | None:
        """Pick a move using weighted random selection.

        Used by BEGINNER difficulty to bias towards captures or
        centre moves while still making occasional blunders.

        Args:
            legal_moves: Non-empty list of valid moves.
            weights: Parallel list of non-negative weights.

        Returns:
            A weighted random move, or ``None``.
        """
        if not legal_moves or not weights:
            return None
        if len(legal_moves) != len(weights):
            return random.choice(legal_moves)
        total = sum(weights)
        if total <= 0:
            return random.choice(legal_moves)
        return random.choices(legal_moves, weights=weights, k=1)[0]

    @staticmethod
    def add_noise(
        scores: list[float],
        noise_level: float = 0.3,
    ) -> list[float]:
        """Add random noise to evaluation scores (for BEGINNER blunders).

        Args:
            scores: List of numeric evaluation scores.
            noise_level: Standard deviation of the Gaussian noise
                as a fraction of the score range.

        Returns:
            Scores with added noise.
        """
        if not scores:
            return scores
        score_range = max(scores) - min(scores) if len(scores) > 1 else 1.0
        if score_range == 0:
            score_range = 1.0
        sigma = noise_level * score_range
        return [s + random.gauss(0, sigma) for s in scores]

#engine/ai/transposition_table.py
# engine/ai/transposition_table.py
"""Zobrist-based transposition table for search acceleration.

Caches previously evaluated positions so the Minimax/Alpha-Beta
engine can skip re-computing them.  Used by the IMPOSSIBLE difficulty.

Usage::

    tt = TranspositionTable(size_mb=64)
    tt.store(board_hash, depth=5, score=1.2, flag=TTFlag.EXACT, best_move=move)
    entry = tt.probe(board_hash)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import IntEnum
from typing import Any


class TTFlag(IntEnum):
    """Transposition table entry type.

    EXACT:      The stored score is the exact minimax value.
    LOWER:      The score is a lower bound (beta cutoff).
    UPPER:      The score is an upper bound (alpha cutoff).
    """

    EXACT = 0
    LOWER = 1
    UPPER = 2


@dataclass(slots=True)
class TTEntry:
    """A single transposition table entry.

    Attributes:
        hash_key: Full Zobrist hash for collision verification.
        depth: Search depth at which this score was computed.
        score: The evaluation score.
        flag: Entry type (EXACT, LOWER, or UPPER).
        best_move: The best move found at this position.
        age: Generation counter for replacement policy.
    """

    hash_key: int = 0
    depth: int = 0
    score: float = 0.0
    flag: TTFlag = TTFlag.EXACT
    best_move: Any = None
    age: int = 0


class TranspositionTable:
    """Fixed-size hash table for caching search results.

    Uses a simple replacement scheme: deeper entries and newer entries
    are preferred over shallow/old ones.

    Attributes:
        capacity: Maximum number of entries.
        generation: Current search generation (incremented each search).
    """

    def __init__(self, size_mb: int = 32) -> None:
        # Estimate ~64 bytes per entry
        self.capacity: int = max(1, (size_mb * 1024 * 1024) // 64)
        self._table: dict[int, TTEntry] = {}
        self.generation: int = 0

        # Stats
        self._hits: int = 0
        self._misses: int = 0
        self._collisions: int = 0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Core Operations
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def store(
        self,
        hash_key: int,
        depth: int,
        score: float,
        flag: TTFlag,
        best_move: Any = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Store or update an entry.

        Replacement policy: always replace if the new entry has
        greater or equal depth, or if the existing entry is from
        a previous generation.

        Args:
            hash_key: Zobrist hash of the position.
            depth: Search depth.
            score: Evaluation score.
            flag: Entry type.
            best_move: Best move at this position.
        """
        index = hash_key % self.capacity
        existing = self._table.get(index)

        if existing is not None:
            # Keep the deeper/newer entry
            if (
                existing.hash_key == hash_key
                and existing.depth > depth
                and existing.age == self.generation
            ):
                return  # Keep existing deeper entry
            if existing.hash_key != hash_key:
                self._collisions += 1

        self._table[index] = TTEntry(
            hash_key=hash_key,
            depth=depth,
            score=score,
            flag=flag,
            best_move=best_move,
            age=self.generation,
        )

    def probe(self, hash_key: int) -> TTEntry | None:
        """Look up an entry by hash.

        Args:
            hash_key: Zobrist hash of the position.

        Returns:
            The matching entry, or ``None`` if not found / collision.
        """
        index = hash_key % self.capacity
        entry = self._table.get(index)

        if entry is None:
            self._misses += 1
            return None

        if entry.hash_key != hash_key:
            self._misses += 1
            return None

        self._hits += 1
        return entry

    def lookup_score(
        self,
        hash_key: int,
        depth: int,
        alpha: float,
        beta: float,
    ) -> float | None:
        """Attempt to use a cached score for alpha-beta pruning.

        Args:
            hash_key: Zobrist hash.
            depth: Minimum required search depth.
            alpha: Current alpha bound.
            beta: Current beta bound.

        Returns:
            The usable score, or ``None`` if the entry can't be used.
        """
        entry = self.probe(hash_key)
        if entry is None:
            return None
        if entry.depth < depth:
            return None

        if entry.flag == TTFlag.EXACT:
            return entry.score
        if entry.flag == TTFlag.LOWER and entry.score >= beta:
            return entry.score
        if entry.flag == TTFlag.UPPER and entry.score <= alpha:
            return entry.score

        return None

    def get_best_move(self, hash_key: int) -> Any:
        """Retrieve the stored best move for move ordering.

        Args:
            hash_key: Zobrist hash.

        Returns:
            The best move, or ``None``.
        """
        entry = self.probe(hash_key)
        if entry is not None:
            return entry.best_move
        return None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Lifecycle
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def new_search(self) -> None:
        """Increment the generation counter before a new search."""
        self.generation += 1

    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Clear all entries and reset stats."""
        self._table.clear()
        self.generation = 0
        self._hits = 0
        self._misses = 0
        self._collisions = 0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Stats
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @property
    def size(self) -> int:
        """Number of entries currently stored."""
        return len(self._table)

    @property
    def hit_rate(self) -> float:
        """Cache hit rate as a fraction."""
        total = self._hits + self._misses
        if total == 0:
            return 0.0
        return self._hits / total

    def get_stats(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Return table statistics.

        Returns:
            Dict with hits, misses, collisions, size, capacity, hit_rate.
        """
        return {
            "hits": self._hits,
            "misses": self._misses,
            "collisions": self._collisions,
            "size": self.size,
            "capacity": self.capacity,
            "hit_rate": self.hit_rate,
        }

#engine/ai_base.py
# engine/ai_base.py
"""Concrete base class providing shared AI plumbing.

``AIBase`` implements the ``AIInterface`` contract and wires in difficulty
management, asynchronous move computation, and event emission so that
game-specific AI subclasses only override the evaluation and search.

Subclasses must implement:
    - ``_select_beginner_move``   — fast, noisy, weak
    - ``_select_hard_move``       — mid-depth alpha-beta or MCTS
    - ``_select_impossible_move`` — tournament-grade search
    - ``evaluate_position``       — heuristic board evaluation
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import random
import threading
from typing import Any, TypeVar

from engine.core.event_manager import get_event_manager
from engine.interfaces.ai_interface import AIInterface
from engine.utils.constants import Difficulty, EventType

_MoveT = TypeVar("_MoveT")


class AIBase(AIInterface):
    """Shared base for all game-specific AI implementations.

    Attributes:
        player: The player id this AI controls.
        difficulty: Current difficulty tier.
        _thinking: ``True`` while async search is in progress.
        _pending_move: The move result from an async search.
        event_manager: For emitting AI thinking events.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        player: int,
        difficulty: Difficulty = Difficulty.BEGINNER,
        is_async: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        self.player: int = player
        self._difficulty: Difficulty = difficulty
        self.is_async: bool = is_async
        self._thinking: bool = False
        self._pending_move: Any | None = None
        self._search_thread: threading.Thread | None = None
        self._search_generation: int = 0
        self._lock: threading.Lock = threading.Lock()
        self.event_manager = get_event_manager()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Lifecycle
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Initialise tables, caches, etc.  Override in subclass."""

    def reset(self) -> None:
        """Clear transient state between games."""
        with self._lock:
            self._search_generation += 1
            if self._thinking:
                self.event_manager.emit(EventType.ON_AI_THINKING_END)
            self._thinking = False
            self._pending_move = None
            self._search_thread = None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Difficulty
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def get_difficulty(self) -> Difficulty:
        return self._difficulty

    def set_difficulty(self, difficulty: Difficulty) -> None:
        if not isinstance(difficulty, Difficulty):
            raise ValueError(f"Expected Difficulty enum, got {type(difficulty)}")
        self._difficulty = difficulty

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Move Selection (public API)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def select_action(self, game_state: dict[str, Any]) -> Any | None:
        """Route to the correct search based on difficulty.

        Args:
            game_state: State dict from ``GameInterface.get_state()``.

        Returns:
            A move, or ``None``.
        """
        legal_moves = game_state.get("legal_moves", [])
        if not legal_moves:
            return None

        if self._difficulty == Difficulty.BEGINNER:
            return self._select_beginner_move(game_state, legal_moves)
        if self._difficulty == Difficulty.EASY:
            return self._select_easy_move(game_state, legal_moves)
        if self._difficulty == Difficulty.MEDIUM:
            return self._select_medium_move(game_state, legal_moves)
        if self._difficulty == Difficulty.HARD:
            return self._select_hard_move(game_state, legal_moves)
        if self._difficulty == Difficulty.IMPOSSIBLE:
            return self._select_impossible_move(game_state, legal_moves)

        return self._select_random_move(legal_moves)

    def get_action(self, game: Any) -> Any | None:
        """Extract state and select a move.

        If `is_async` is True, automatically dispatches search to a background
        worker thread on first call, returns None while searching (allowing UI
        to render at 60 FPS), and returns the computed move as soon as the
        background worker completes.

        If `is_async` is False (e.g. during headless automated testing),
        executes synchronously and returns the move immediately.

        Args:
            game: A ``GameInterface`` instance.

        Returns:
            A move, or ``None`` while searching.
        """
        if game is None or game.is_game_over():
            return None

        # Return pending async result if available (thread-safe)
        with self._lock:
            if self._pending_move is not None:
                move = self._pending_move
                self._pending_move = None
                self.event_manager.emit(EventType.ON_AI_THINKING_END)
                return move  # type: ignore[no-any-return]

            if self._thinking:
                return None

        # If synchronous mode requested, compute directly
        if not self.is_async:
            return self.select_action(game.get_state())

        # Start non-blocking background search thread and return None for this tick
        self.select_action_async(game.get_state())
        return None

    def get_move(self, game: Any) -> Any | None:
        """Alias for get_action."""
        return self.get_action(game)

    def compute_move(self, game: Any) -> Any | None:
        """Alias for AIInterface contract."""
        return self.get_action(game)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Async Search
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def select_action_async(
        self,
        game_state: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> None:
        """Start a background thread for move computation.

        The result is stored in ``_pending_move`` and picked up by
        ``get_action`` on the next frame tick.

        Args:
            game_state: State dict.
        """
        with self._lock:
            if self._thinking:
                return
            self._thinking = True
            current_gen = self._search_generation

        self.event_manager.emit(EventType.ON_AI_THINKING_START)

        def _worker() -> None:
            try:
                move = self.select_action(game_state)
                with self._lock:
                    if self._search_generation == current_gen:
                        self._pending_move = move
            except Exception as exc:
                import logging

                logging.getLogger(__name__).exception("Error in AI async worker: %s", exc)
            finally:
                with self._lock:
                    if self._search_generation == current_gen:
                        self._thinking = False
                        if self._pending_move is None:
                            self.event_manager.emit(EventType.ON_AI_THINKING_END)

        self._search_thread = threading.Thread(target=_worker, daemon=True)
        self._search_thread.start()

    @property
    def is_thinking(self) -> bool:
        """Return ``True`` if an async search is in progress."""
        with self._lock:
            return self._thinking

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Difficulty-Specific Hooks (subclass must override)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _select_beginner_move(
        self,
        game_state: dict[str, Any],
        legal_moves: list[Any],
    ) -> Any | None:
        """Select a weak/noisy move.

        Default implementation: random choice.

        Args:
            game_state: Full state dict.
            legal_moves: Pre-filtered list of valid moves.

        Returns:
            A move.
        """
        return self._select_random_move(legal_moves)

    def _select_easy_move(
        self,
        game_state: dict[str, Any],
        legal_moves: list[Any],
    ) -> Any | None:
        """Select a slightly better-than-random move.

        Default implementation: 70% chance of beginner move,
        30% chance of hard move. Subclass can override.

        Args:
            game_state: Full state dict.
            legal_moves: Pre-filtered list of valid moves.

        Returns:
            A move.
        """
        if random.random() < 0.7:
            return self._select_beginner_move(game_state, legal_moves)
        return self._select_hard_move(game_state, legal_moves)

    def _select_medium_move(
        self,
        game_state: dict[str, Any],
        legal_moves: list[Any],
    ) -> Any | None:
        """Select an intermediate-strength move.

        Default implementation: 30% chance of beginner move,
        70% chance of hard move. Subclass can override.

        Args:
            game_state: Full state dict.
            legal_moves: Pre-filtered list of valid moves.

        Returns:
            A move.
        """
        if random.random() < 0.3:
            return self._select_beginner_move(game_state, legal_moves)
        return self._select_hard_move(game_state, legal_moves)

    def _select_hard_move(
        self,
        game_state: dict[str, Any],
        legal_moves: list[Any],
    ) -> Any | None:
        """Select a mid-strength move (alpha-beta / MCTS).

        Default implementation: random choice (override in subclass).

        Args:
            game_state: Full state dict.
            legal_moves: Pre-filtered list of valid moves.

        Returns:
            A move.
        """
        return self._select_random_move(legal_moves)

    def _select_impossible_move(
        self,
        game_state: dict[str, Any],
        legal_moves: list[Any],
    ) -> Any | None:
        """Select a tournament-grade move.

        Default implementation: random choice (override in subclass).

        Args:
            game_state: Full state dict.
            legal_moves: Pre-filtered list of valid moves.

        Returns:
            A move.
        """
        return self._select_random_move(legal_moves)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Utility
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @staticmethod
    def _select_random_move(legal_moves: list[_MoveT]) -> _MoveT | None:
        """Choose a uniformly random legal move.

        Args:
            legal_moves: Non-empty list of moves.

        Returns:
            A randomly chosen move, or ``None`` if list is empty.
        """
        if not legal_moves:
            return None
        return random.choice(legal_moves)

    def set_player(self, player: int) -> None:
        """Change the player this AI controls.

        Args:
            player: New player id.
        """
        self.player = player

    def get_player(self) -> int:
        """Return the player id this AI controls."""
        return self.player

//engine/rendering/shaders/spotlight.frag
// engine/rendering/shaders/spotlight.frag
#version 330 core

in vec3 frag_world_pos;
in vec3 frag_normal;
in vec2 frag_uv;

uniform vec3 spotlight_pos;       // e.g. (0.0, 0.0, 9.5)
uniform vec3 spotlight_dir;       // e.g. (0.0, 0.0, -1.0)
uniform float spotlight_cutoff;   // cos(inner_angle)
uniform float spotlight_outer;    // cos(outer_angle)
uniform vec3 spotlight_color;     // e.g. (1.0, 0.96, 0.88)
uniform vec3 ambient_color;       // e.g. (0.03, 0.03, 0.05) - dark room
uniform vec4 base_color;          // Surface tint
uniform vec3 camera_pos;          // For specular & rim lighting
uniform float rim_intensity;      // Rim-light multiplier

out vec4 fragColor;

void main()
{
    vec3 norm = normalize(frag_normal);
    vec3 light_dir = normalize(spotlight_pos - frag_world_pos);
    vec3 view_dir = normalize(camera_pos - frag_world_pos);

    // 1. Spotlight Cone attenuation
    float theta = dot(light_dir, normalize(-spotlight_dir));
    float epsilon = spotlight_cutoff - spotlight_outer;
    float spot_intensity = clamp((theta - spotlight_outer) / max(epsilon, 0.001), 0.0, 1.0);

    // Distance attenuation
    float dist = length(spotlight_pos - frag_world_pos);
    float atten = 1.0 / (1.0 + 0.04 * dist + 0.012 * dist * dist);

    // 2. Diffuse shading
    float diff = max(dot(norm, light_dir), 0.0);
    vec3 diffuse = diff * spotlight_color * spot_intensity * atten;

    // 3. Specular highlight (polished table / marble / stones)
    vec3 halfway_dir = normalize(light_dir + view_dir);
    float spec = pow(max(dot(norm, halfway_dir), 0.0), 32.0);
    vec3 specular = spec * spotlight_color * 0.45 * spot_intensity * atten;

    // 4. Dramatic Rim Lighting (silhouette edge glow)
    float rim = 1.0 - max(dot(view_dir, norm), 0.0);
    rim = pow(rim, 3.0) * rim_intensity;
    vec3 rim_glow = rim * spotlight_color;

    // Final composition
    vec3 total_light = ambient_color + diffuse + specular + rim_glow;
    fragColor = vec4(base_color.rgb * total_light, base_color.a);
}

//engine/rendering/shaders/spotlight.vert
// engine/rendering/shaders/spotlight.vert
#version 330 core

in vec3 in_position;
in vec3 in_normal;
in vec2 in_uv;

uniform mat4 projection;
uniform mat4 view;
uniform mat4 model;

out vec3 frag_world_pos;
out vec3 frag_normal;
out vec2 frag_uv;

void main()
{
    vec4 world_pos = model * vec4(in_position, 1.0);
    frag_world_pos = world_pos.xyz;
    frag_normal = normalize(mat3(transpose(inverse(model))) * in_normal);
    frag_uv = in_uv;

    gl_Position = projection * view * world_pos;
}

//engine/rendering/shaders/text.frag
//engine/rendering/shaders/text.frag
#version 330
in vec2 uv;
out vec4 fragColor;
uniform sampler2D text_texture;
void main()
{
    fragColor = texture(text_texture,uv);
}
